# Commission Delegated Decision (EU) 2026/1814 of 24 July 2026 amending and correcting Directive 2005/36/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council as regards evidence of formal qualifications and the titles of training courses (notified under document C(2026) 5051)

> **This is the law as enacted. Amendments are not incorporated below — each one is
> a separate file in this repository and a commit in this file's history.**

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Image omitted; see official source](https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/?uri=CELEX:32026D1814) | Official Journal of the European Union | EN L series |

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
|  | 2026/1814 | 9.11.2026 |

THE EUROPEAN COMMISSION,

Having regard to the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union,

Having regard to Directive 2005/36/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 7 September 2005 on the recognition of professional qualifications [^ntr1-L_202601814EN.000101-E0001], and in particular Article 21a(4) thereof,

Whereas:

- (1) Annex V to Directive 2005/36/EC contains lists with evidence of formal qualifications of doctors of medicine, nurses responsible for general care, dental practitioners, veterinary surgeons, midwives, pharmacists and architects.

- (2) Several EU countries have notified the Commission of legislative, regulatory and administrative provisions they have adopted on issuing evidence of formal qualifications covered by Chapter III of Title III of Directive 2005/36/EC.

- (3) The Commission has assessed EU countries’ notifications and concluded that provisions notified by EU countries comply with the conditions set out in Articles 24, 25, 28, 31, 34, 35, 38, 40, 41, 44 and 46 of Directive 2005/36/EC. The outcome of the assessment of those notifications should result in amendments to Annex V to Directive 2005/36/EC.

- (4) Following the notification from Italy, Commission Delegated Decision (EU) 2021/2183 [^ntr2-L_202601814EN.000101-E0002] added *Biochimica* as the title of a training course in specialised medicine to point 5.1.3 of Annex V to Directive 2005/36/EC. In line with the explanations from Italy, this title should not have been added to Directive 2005/36/EC. That error should therefore be corrected by deleting the title from Annex V to Directive 2005/36/EC.

- (5) Annex V to Directive 2005/36/EC should therefore be amended and corrected accordingly. For reasons of clarity and legal certainty, all relevant points of Annex V to Directive 2005/36/EC on evidence of formal qualifications and the titles of training courses, this is to say, points 5.1.1 to 5.1.4, 5.2.2., 5.3.2, 5.3.3, 5.4.2, 5.5.2, 5.6.2 and 5.7.1, should be replaced.

HAS ADOPTED THIS DECISION:

#### Article 1

Annex V to Directive 2005/36/EC is amended and corrected in accordance with the Annex to this Decision.

#### Article 2

This Decision is addressed to the Member States.

Done at Brussels, 24 July 2026.

***For the Commission***

**Roxana MÎNZATU**

***Executive Vice-President***

[^ntr2-L_202601814EN.000101-E0002]: Commission Delegated Decision (EU) 2021/2183 of 25 August 2021 amending Annex V to Directive 2005/36/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council as regards the evidence of formal qualifications and titles of training courses ([OJ L 444, 10.12.2021, p. 16](http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_2021_444_R_TOC), ELI: [http://data.europa.eu/eli/dec_del/2021/2183/oj](http://data.europa.eu/eli/dec_del/2021/2183/oj)).

## ANNEX

Annex V to Directive 2005/36/EC is amended as follows:

- 1. points 5.1.1 to 5.1.4 are replaced by the following:

> | United Kingdom | 1. Diplomas in architecture[^ntr301-L_202601814EN.000301-E0301] | 1. — Universities | Certificate of architectural education, issued by the Architects Registration Board. The diploma and degree courses in architecture of the universities, schools and colleges of art should have met the requisite threshold standards as laid down in Article 46 of this Directive and in Criteria for validation published by the Validation Panel of the Royal Institute of British Architects and the Architects Registration Board. EU nationals who possess the Royal Institute of British Architects Part I and Part II certificates, which are recognised by ARB as the competent authority, are eligible. Also EU nationals who do not possess the ARB-recognised Part I and Part II certificates will be eligible for the Certificate of Architectural Education if they can satisfy the Board that their standard and length of education has met the requisite threshold standards of Article 46 of this Directive and of the Criteria for validation. An Architects Registration Board Part 3 Certificate of Architectural Education | 1988/1989 |

[^ntr311-L_202601814EN.000301-E0311]: This university awarded the ‘diploma de arhitect’ to students who started the training in the reference academic year 2010/2011 only (diplomas were awarded until 2015/2016), and then again to students who started the training in the reference academic year 2024/2025.

[^ntr312-L_202601814EN.000301-E0312]: This university awarded the ‘diploma de arhitect’ to students who started the training in the reference academic year 2010/2011 only (diplomas were awarded until 2015/2016), and then again to students who started the training in the reference academic year 2024/2025.
